The full CV bootstrap with 10000 iterations of 5-fold CV across 36 layers is indeed expensive. \n\nLet me estimate: each cv_auroc call processes 36 layers × 5 folds = 180 AUROC computations. With 181 pairs, each AUROC computation involves ~72 scores (36 test × 2 conditions). The scoring involves (n_test, 36, 4096) * (36, 4096) dot products.\n\n\nThe real bottleneck is the numpy array indexing that creates copies of the full data for each bootstrap iteration—we're duplicating about 214 MB per iteration across 10000 iterations, which means the memory operations alone are killing performance. At 0.5-1 second per iteration, this whole process would take 1.5-3 hours, which is way too long.\n\nI could optimize by restricting to just the layers where we're seeing meaningful results (15-35), which would significantly reduce the data being copied each iteration. But actually, I'm reconsidering whether the full CV bootstrap is even necessary here—the OOF bootstrap is already giving us confidence intervals, and for responding to the reviewer, that should be sufficient. Let me run the first 1000 iterations to see how long it actually takes, then decide whether to optimize or just let it finish.","signature":"EocPCmMIDRgCKkAN8drqT3DYD2XsKU7HhVe9nhA2FRWbv0vlllpsIAB55oDtAOItwXLg199dBZdD3ON8HILjftemD2K/8U5hDrPgMg9jbGF1ZGUtb3B1cy00LTY4AEIIdGhpbmtpbmcSDF58pcH3gIDLisWvFRoMtSqHCQBp7T+vcu9iIjBM5y9s7Xp4bhug/c87/Ytkl4Lx7UjKvrWTjJabfs1jY17c6xVWWGMIc21MpEc+zD0q0Q2MIQDS4CYiCYd9zrgCOZLflrJNUvvnH/WQG9pNWnfYRAwUalRK6RgRhvX7NpBBfFmTIXvSm+AoPgnRZkJf27ZN9L6YAg31ELPLCiT7pUcXXJSPQEuMFKBXuDVS3C/JQ4tHiR1bhC0+nm16lhkEDz4vmaTqAJC9JGxFMQ+6huej1G2if0yPZuirzfaXCwLZnRLmWl2U6KHlCP+0zAqMz40XdzP5Typ4skifaEQMdfYpjl7My8+39CrL66HXJlo3tcSLsnBljvaz/GQwAQlfQD5MCLez0KkD1BQHgwr2obiXv7imryZxyp7wmKHIQtJon1kxVLKpljpu4g2XHL+e013ZMLvoULdmCjG4Z+r4gMnX3+D4yMzYDC5T4saZwD7NFA/0/rUVYdMLze4qEH5HkkzS4h3w0u43FIhe2pj9v7JAuSL5olIiCoL+0XPl5RC1m5mn3+w5RSmdfhXN0f0eDjVXjqKJeWEngkUnj2LYCafzjLJcKV7lQjhDtRxIIs5SG0t1jlf1aXdU9+bNnEdh6MnAcJZMwb6LVEC54/lL+6dteTUtUVC8EpaeKWuM3bBf2fdp/yfmLlk89uOSyoICimI19e212bwWHFMyUSK38ljmMOg2tzYQRzsnYdhhheT/FPgr3Bw7sYTTJoTAQdjqqJwzY293206yy2k7THv8yI55EZK910O+1n+6fhR6FYfITT0wS8v/cLSwojYCsKq5UmRy8etiDEngkqsix7Mv5DwzJJn739DEB+z2cfYmbUFY8pWM0gvApi85SJNDV4B1uEn06OwPC+27ShtkD4eXswSu6tTa61v2H7tNPmtzGy8inU6W+morgk1rmTpRfTpRFcSNUqigm+b/QPuDGzAhnFUhEfdK2ooKaakJbYpnrltyFzw/9WV5RKOvhwBtFeC7fYzPl34mP0ejf2raxklzO9j4XEWINg3MpirgGSUSOZhKRMr5TfIoux1N1SSAYGDx4Dg/CsYSxOo8awzQVpa46foVozbl9eYjIVdIMGCmTxzhuVIPCLd2cBsCsxbhx2VLwcq5vkEd5h2SwK04OgmyPJ90NfGb9HXWGON28pY+v3Dp173Ml1pH0YueXdcHVF3ZZ40q/mz2vzSAdMvQkY0FVRjvG84iFk4eq+Q4BJK9pYalVM77ENjIJv/fRTMIC2GKCKcghyAc6cl31yT85B9H7wCp/7MowH4ngel1vwk2v6UkRxKHIbvbmkVjuhsWfNrjHn+3vEefFA6GdptfSgiIX68pNBy04+3qH9Xd4/0tqofrSuSuVziiJNkEb+gwIEh8DDvU7fwc/+wobYnzNEPmARRYWp9eCfISEElvTmI7F5Pf1dJJniJxVORpOqdTl9SafWhd2wxTofl9VluGkqNv7D7q21mnWCVLGffMerW0Z0vFIEkMpaUswLt5RwC2r59lAJqmB4cQCv410LiEL2qXGx28kTU5B/4RkVc4ESXy9DK7ZNWgXmL944+Q5rNAkpLnifAyGgFot5ZRe9B5OuUHK38kVwNtWget55MlCQBlIpuwjDOEoxuFcFZgcRXJknP+nRbRV/DpNerR7bf2j+pyBHw4UXX9Gnz4gKUNKJ7XCI0Z1+lby9umTs3zdMGa7VLZGyVhPYrDJof9LSRDd/kgSFlOrqnoDT5cA2VUt0o3iKCG4kuCurK7WY7ySpY8KpvplMx25snj7Guo+hfPtmv4p7Vy+gPW+dKMDQ47HsXpA1uvCu9qCV Looking at the bootstrap_cv_auroc function:\n\n```python\ndef bootstrap_cv_auroc(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP,\n                       seed=SEED, layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None):\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    n_pairs = syc.shape[0]\n    aurocs = []\n    for b in range(n_bootstrap):\n        boot_idx = rng.choice(n_pairs, size=n_pairs, replace=True)\n\n\n```\n\nDefining the bootstrap loop...\n\nEach iteration resamples the data, handles domain stratification by falling back to modulo assignment if needed, then runs the full cross-validation pipeline. \\\"#!/usr/bin/env python3\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Bootstrap 95% CI for d_syc AUROC metrics.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nimport json, sys, time\\\\nfrom collections import defaultdict\\\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nimport torch\\\\nfrom sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\\\\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\\\\n\\\\nN_BOOTSTRAP = 10000\\\\nN_FOLDS = 5\\\\nSEED = 42\\\\n\\\\ndef load_pairs(path, act_key=\\\\\\\"activations\\\\\\\"):\\\\n    records = torch.load(path, map_location=\\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\", weights_only=False)\\\\n    pairs = defaultdict(dict)\\\\n    for s in records:\\\\n        pairs[s[\\\\\\\"pair_id\\\\\\\"]][s[\\\\\\\"condition\\\\\\\"]] = s\\\\n    return {pid: c for pid, c in pairs.items()\\\\n            if \\\\\\\"valid_correction\\\\\\\" in c and \\\\\\\"invalid_pressure\\\\\\\" in c}\\\\n\\\\ndef prepare_arrays(pairs, act_key=\\\\\\\"activations\\\\\\\", pos=None):\\\\n    pair_ids = sorted(pairs.keys())\\\\n    syc_list, wr_list, domains, strengths = [], [], [], []\\\\n    for pid in pair_ids:\\\\n        s_act = pairs[pid][\\\\\\\"invalid_pressure\\\\\\\"][act_key]\\\\n        w_act = pairs[pid][\\\\\\\"valid_correction\\\\\\\"][act_key]\\\\n        if pos is not None:\\\\n            s_act = s_act[:, pos, :]\\\\n            w_act = w_act[:, pos, :]\\\\n        syc_list.append(s_act)\\\\n        wr_list.append(w_act)\\\\n        domains.append(pairs[pid][\\\\\\\"valid_correction\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"domain\\\\\\\"])\\\\n        strengths.append(pairs[pid][\\\\\\\"valid_correction\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"evidence_strength\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    syc = torch.stack(syc_list).numpy().astype(np.float32)\\\\n    wr = torch.stack(wr_list).numpy().astype(np.float32)\\\\n    return syc, wr, domains, strengths, pair_ids\\\\n\\\\ndef cv_auroc(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_folds=N_FOLDS, seed=SEED,\\\\n             layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None, return_oof=False):\\\\n    n_pairs = syc.shape[0]\\\\n    if layer_hi is None:\\\\n        layer_hi = syc.shape[1]\\\\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_folds, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\\\\n    fold_aurocs = []\\\\n    oof_syc = np.zeros(n_pairs)\\\\n    oof_wr = np.zeros(n_pairs)\\\\n    for train_idx, test_idx in skf.split(np.arange(n_pairs), domains_arr):\\\\n        d = syc[train_idx].mean(axis=0) - wr[train_idx].mean(axis=0)\\\\n        norms = np.linalg.norm(d, axis=-1, keepdims=True)\\\\n        d = d / np.maximum(norms, 1e-8)\\\\n        s_test = (syc[test_idx] * d).sum(axis=-1)\\\\n        w_test = (wr[test_idx] * d).sum(axis=-1)\\\\n        best_auroc = -1.0\\\\n        best_layer = layer_lo\\\\n        for layer in range(layer_lo, layer_hi):\\\\n            scores = np.concatenate([s_test[:, layer], w_test[:, layer]])\\\\n            labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(test_idx)), np.zeros(len(test_idx))])\\\\n            try:\\\\n                auroc = roc_auc_score(labels, scores)\\\\n                if auroc > best_auroc:\\\\n                    best_auroc = auroc\\\\n                    best_layer = layer\\\\n            except ValueError:\\\\n                pass\\\\n        fold_aurocs.append(best_auroc)\\\\n        oof_syc[test_idx] = s_test[:, best_layer]\\\\n        oof_wr[test_idx] = w_test[:, best_layer]\\\\n    mean_auroc = float(np.mean(fold_aurocs))\\\\n    if return_oof:\\\\n        return mean_auroc, fold_aurocs, oof_syc, oof_wr\\\\n    return mean_auroc, fold_aurocs\\\\n\\\\ndef bootstrap_cv_auroc(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP,\\\\n                       seed=SEED, layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None):\\\\n    rng = bootstrap_oof_auroc(oof_syc, oof_wr, mask=None,\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.44}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"#!/usr/bin/env python3\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"Bootstrap 95% CI for d_syc AUROC metrics.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport json, sys, time\\nfrom collections import defaultdict\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\\n\\nN_BOOTSTRAP = 10000\\nN_FOLDS = 5\\nSEED = 42\\n\\ndef load_pairs(path, act_key=\\\"activations\\\"):\\n    records = torch.load(path, map_location=\\\"cpu\\\", weights_only=False)\\n    pairs = defaultdict(dict)\\n    for s in records:\\n        pairs[s[\\\"pair_id\\\"]][s[\\\"condition\\\"]] = s\\n    return {pid: c for pid, c in pairs.items()\\n            if \\\"valid_correction\\\" in c and \\\"invalid_pressure\\\" in c}\\n\\ndef prepare_arrays(pairs, act_key=\\\"activations\\\", pos=None):\\n    pair_ids = sorted(pairs.keys())\\n    syc_list, wr_list, domains, strengths = [], [], [], []\\n    for pid in pair_ids:\\n        s_act = pairs[pid][\\\"invalid_pressure\\\"][act_key]\\n        w_act = pairs[pid][\\\"valid_correction\\\"][act_key]\\n        if pos is not None:\\n            s_act = s_act[:, pos, :]\\n            w_act = w_act[:, pos, :]\\n        syc_list.append(s_act)\\n        wr_list.append(w_act)\\n        domains.append(pairs[pid][\\\"valid_correction\\\"][\\\"domain\\\"])\\n        strengths.append(pairs[pid][\\\"valid_correction\\\"][\\\"evidence_strength\\\"])\\n    syc = torch.stack(syc_list).numpy().astype(np.float32)\\n    wr = torch.stack(wr_list).numpy().astype(np.float32)\\n    return syc, wr, domains, strengths, pair_ids\\n\\ndef cv_auroc(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_folds=N_FOLDS, seed=SEED,\\n             layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None, return_oof=False):\\n    n_pairs = syc.shape[0]\\n    if layer_hi is None:\\n        layer_hi = syc.shape[1]\\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_folds, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\\n    fold_aurocs = []\\n    oof_syc = np.zeros(n_pairs)\\n    oof_wr = np.zeros(n_pairs)\\n    for train_idx, test_idx in skf.split(np.arange(n_pairs), domains_arr):\\n        d = syc[train_idx].mean(axis=0) - wr[train_idx].mean(axis=0)\\n        norms = np.linalg.norm(d, axis=-1, keepdims=True)\\n        d = d / np.maximum(norms, 1e-8)\\n        s_test = (syc[test_idx] * d).sum(axis=-1)\\n        w_test = (wr[test_idx] * d).sum(axis=-1)\\n        best_auroc = -1.0\\n        best_layer = layer_lo\\n        for layer in range(layer_lo, layer_hi):\\n            scores = np.concatenate([s_test[:, layer], w_test[:, layer]])\\n            labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(test_idx)), np.zeros(len(test_idx))])\\n            try:\\n                auroc = roc_auc_score(labels, scores)\\n                if auroc > best_auroc:\\n                    best_auroc = auroc\\n                    best_layer = layer\\n            except ValueError:\\n                pass\\n        fold_aurocs.append(best_auroc)\\n        oof_syc[test_idx] = s_test[:, best_layer]\\n        oof_wr[test_idx] = w_test[:, best_layer]\\n    mean_auroc = float(np.mean(fold_aurocs))\\n    if return_oof:\\n        return mean_auroc, fold_aurocs, oof_syc, oof_wr\\n    return mean_auroc, fold_aurocs\\n\\ndef bootstrap_cv_auroc(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP,\\n                       seed=SEED, layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None):\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    n_pairs = syc.shape[0]\\n    aurocs = []\\n    for b in range(n_bootstrap):\\n        boot_idx = rng.choice(n_pairs, size=n_pairs, replace=True)\\n        boot_domains = domains_arr[boot_idx]\\n        if len(np.unique(boot_domains)) < N_FOLDS:\\n            boot_domains = np.arange(n_pairs) % N_FOLDS\\n        try:\\n            mean_auc, _ = cv_auroc(\\n                syc[boot_idx], wr[boot_idx], boot_domains,\\n                seed=b, layer_lo=layer_lo, layer_hi=layer_hi)\\n            aurocs.append(mean_auc)\\n        except Exception:\\n            pass\\n        if (b+1) % 1000 == 0:\\n            print(f\\\"  bootstrap {b+1}/{n_bootstrap}...\\\", flush=True)\\n    aurocs = np.array(aurocs)\\n    return {\\\"mean\\\": float(np.mean(aurocs)), \\\"std\\\": float(np.std(aurocs)),\\n            \\\"ci_95_lower\\\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 2.5)),\\n            \\\"ci_95_upper\\\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 97.5)),\\n            \\\"n_valid\\\": len(aurocs)}\\n\\ndef bootstrap_oof_auroc(oof_syc, oof_wr, mask=None,\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_012MzDS49TVNcUimX6w6GNmq","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"6+ minutes and still no first-1000 progress print. The full CV bootstrap is really expensive. Let me estimate more carefully. \n\nIn the cv_auroc function, for each fold:\n1. d = syc[train_idx].mean(axis=0) - wr[train_idx].mean(axis=0) — mean of ~145 arrays of shape (36, 4096) → shape (36, 4096)\n2. Normalize: 36 norms, each 4096D\n3. s_test = (syc[test_idx] * d).sum(axis=-1) — broadcasting (36, 36, 4096) * (36, 4096), then sum → (36, 36) — wait that's wrong.\n\n\n4. Actually syc[test_idx] is (n_test, 36, 4096) and d is (36, 4096), so the product gives (n_test, 36, 4096) and summing over the last axis yields (n_test, 36), which is correct.\n\nFor n_test ≈ 36, that's about 21 MB for the product array alone, then I'm looping through 21 layers computing ROC-AUC scores on 72 values each, and this happens 5 times per bootstrap.\n\nThe real issue is that each bootstrap iteration does fancy indexing into the arrays—syc[boot_idx] creates a ~100 MB copy, and then inside the cross-validation loop, each fold creates additional copies (85 MB for training, 21 MB for testing) plus intermediate arrays. With 10,000 iterations, the cumulative memory allocation and garbage collection overhead becomes the bottleneck.\n\nGiven that each iteration probably takes 0.3-0.5 seconds, 1000 iterations would run for 5-8 minutes, which explains why the first progress output hasn't appeared yet. I should wait a bit longer to see if it starts
